    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

  "task_prompt": "## 任务：分析 14B SFT eval 结果\n\n实验 `plan017_14b_sft_eval_r2` 已完成（进程正常退出），需要提取 eval metrics。\n\n### 背景\n- 实验：14B SFT baseline eval (bf16, --no-quantize)，修正 r1 的 D076 违规（r1 用了 int8 量化）\n- 服务器：rtx5090-4x\n- 命令：`bash scripts/14b/eval_14b_sft.sh`\n- GPU：cuda:1\n- checkpoint_source: /root/autodl-tmp/sft_output_14b/\n- eval_data: /root/autodl-tmp/data/docred/dev.json\n- 14B 是第 4 个 scale 档，SFT baseline 用于计算 RSFT 增益和 evidence tax\n- 预期 ign_f1 高于 8B SFT mean (0.4866)\n\n### 你需要做的\n1. SSH 到 rtx5090-4x，找到 eval 输出日志。可能的位置：\n   - 标准输出重定向的日志文件（检查 /root/autodl-tmp/ 或项目目录下的 logs/ 目录）\n   - tmux session 的输出\n   - 检查 `ls -lt /root/autodl-tmp/sft_output_14b/` 看有没有 eval 结果文件\n   - 检查 `/root/autodl-tmp/faith-evidence-ie/` 或类似项目目录下的日志\n   - 用 `find /root/autodl-tmp/ -name \"*14b*eval*\" -o -name \"*metrics*\" -mmin -120 2>/dev/null | head -20` 搜索近期文件\n   - 检查 tmux: `tmux ls` 然后 `tmux capture-pane -t &lt;session> -p -S -500`\n\n2. 提取关键 metrics：\n   - ign_f1, ign_precision, ign_recall（最重要）\n   - f1, precision, recall\n   - format_compliance_rate\n   - n_truncated\n   - inference_time_seconds\n   - 如果有 evidence 相关指标也提取：evi_precision, evi_recall, evi_f1, edcr\n\n3. 找到 checkpoint_path 和 log_path 的确切位置\n\n4. 将所有提取的 metrics 用 `update_registry` 写入：\n   ```\n   update_registry(exp_id=\"plan017_14b_sft_eval_r2\", field=\"metrics\", value={...}, project=\"faith-evidence-ie\")\n   ```\n\n5. 判断 conclusion：\n   - 如果 ign_f1 > 0.4866（8B SFT mean），说明 14B 确实比 8B 强 → conclusion = \"success\"\n   - 如果 ign_f1 ≤ 0.4866，说明 14B 没有如预期提升 → conclusion = \"negative\"\n   \n6. 设置 conclusion：\n   ```\n   update_registry(exp_id=\"plan017_14b_sft_eval_r2\", field=\"conclusion\", value=\"success\"或\"negative\", project=\"faith-evidence-ie\")\n   ```\n\n7. 设置 checkpoint_path 和 log_path：\n   ```\n   update_registry(exp_id=\"plan017_14b_sft_eval_r2\", field=\"spec\", value={\"checkpoint_path\": \"...\", \"log_path\": \"...\"}, project=\"faith-evidence-ie\", spec={\"checkpoint_path\": \"...\", \"log_path\": \"...\"})\n   ```\n\n### 对比参考\n- 14B SFT no-evidence eval (plan017_14b_sft_no_evidence_eval_r2): ign_f1=0.551\n- 8B SFT mean: 0.4866 (s42=0.5128, s44=0.4604)\n- 4B SFT mean: 0.4358\n- 1.7B SFT mean: 0.4464\n\n### 重要\n- 只用 ssh_read_only 读取日志和结果，不要用 ssh_execute\n- 完成后确保 registry 中 metrics、conclusion、checkpoint_path、log_path 都已更新",

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_6/projects/faith-evidence-ie/memory.md\", \"content\": \"# Project Memory\\n\\n## RSFT 方差压缩效应 (2026-05-19)\\n\\n**发现**: RSFT 在所有尺度显著压缩跨 seed 方差，且压缩倍数随模型容量递减：\\n\\n| Scale | SFT std | RSFT std | 压缩倍数 | SFT n | RSFT n |\\n|-------|---------|----------|---------|-------|--------|\\n| 1.7B | 2.79pp | 0.09pp | **31x** | 3 | 3 |\\n| 4B | 3.80pp | 0.51pp | **7.5x** | 3 | 3 |\\n| 8B | 2.62pp | 0.53pp | **5x** | 2 | 2 |\\n\\n**解释**: RSFT 的 CED 筛选过程相当于对训练数据施加 quality filter，减少了因随机种子导致的训练轨迹分歧。小模型更容易受数据噪声影响，所以压缩效应最强(31x)。大模型本身已较稳定(SFT std 较低)，RSFT 仍能进一步压缩(5x)。\\n\\n**论文意义**: 这是 RSFT 独立于 ign_f1 提升的第二个贡献维度——训练稳定性。可作为 Claim 1 的补充叙事：\\\"RSFT not only improves performance but dramatically stabilizes training outcomes across random seeds.\\\"\\n\\n**注意**: 1.7B RSFT 所有 3 seeds 使用 Method A（s42 SFT base + s42 CED data，仅变 RSFT seed），压缩效应测量的是 RSFT 训练过程本身的稳定性，不含 SFT base 变异。8B RSFT 使用相同方法（s42 SFT base），但仅 2 seeds。\\n\\n## plan_021: Deep Error Analysis — RSFT vs SFT (2026-05-19)\\n\\n**Files**: `scripts/analysis/deep_error_analysis.py` → `scripts/analysis/results/`\\n\\n**Models compared** (all dev set, seed 42 eval):\\n- 4B SFT: `d111_4b_sft_s42_reeval` (ign_f1=0.397)\\n- 4B RSFT: `rsft_s43_dev_eval_preds` (train seed 43, ign_f1=0.451) — NOTE: s42 predictions unavailable, used s43 model\\n- 8B SFT: `qwen3_8b_sft_eval` (ign_f1=0.505)\\n- 8B RSFT: `d102_8b_k1_s42` (ign_f1=0.538)\\n\\n### Key Findings\\n\\n**Per-Relation F1 (171 unique relations in predictions)**:\\n- 4B largest gains: original network (+0.32, gold=38), award received (+0.16, gold=66), cast member (+0.14, gold=225), performer (+0.13, gold=320)\\n- 4B largest losses: chairperson (-0.16, gold=21), screenwriter (-0.09, gold=35), genre (-0.09, gold=14)\\n- Pattern: RSFT helps most on mid-frequency entertainment/media relations where SFT under-performs. Losses concentrated on low-frequency relations (gold < 40).\\n- 8B gains are more spread out: genre (+0.34), present in work (+0.21), mother (+0.19), league (+0.17)\\n- 8B losses: sibling (-0.16), dissolved/abolished (-0.15), parent organization (-0.14), parent taxon (-0.19)\\n\\n**Per-Document-Length (ign_f1, quantiles: short ≤160, mid 161-208, long >208 words)**:\\n| Bucket | 4B SFT | 4B RSFT | 4B gain | 8B SFT | 8B RSFT | 8B gain |\\n|--------|--------|---------|---------|--------|---------|---------|\\n| short  | 0.416  | 0.472   | +0.056  | 0.551  | 0.580   | +0.029  |\\n| mid    | 0.427  | 0.486   | +0.058  | 0.520  | 0.543   | +0.023  |\\n| long   | 0.349  | 0.398   | +0.048  | 0.445  | 0.490   | +0.046  |\\n\\n- 4B: gain relatively uniform (~5pt), slightly less on long docs\\n- 8B: gain is **largest on long docs** (+4.6pt vs +2.3-2.9pt on short/mid) — RSFT helps 8B more on harder/longer documents\\n\\n**Per-Entity-Count (ign_f1, quantiles: low ≤17, mid 18-22, high >22 entities)**:\\n| Bucket | 4B SFT | 4B RSFT | 4B gain | 8B SFT | 8B RSFT | 8B gain |\\n|--------|--------|---------|---------|--------|---------|---------|\\n| low    | 0.432  | 0.475   | +0.044  | 0.534  | 0.574   | +0.040  |\\n| mid    | 0.398  | 0.462   | +0.064  | 0.523  | 0.543   | +0.020  |\\n| high   | 0.369  | 0.423   | +0.054  | 0.466  | 0.503   | +0.037  |\\n\\n- 4B: mid-entity docs gain most (+6.4pt); low-entity least (+4.4pt)\\n- 8B: low-entity and high-entity gain similarly (~4pt); mid-entity gains least (+2.0pt)\\n\\n### Interpretation\\n1. RSFT consistently improves across all stratifications — no systematic \\\"only helps easy cases\\\"\\n2. For 4B, gain is fairly uniform across complexity dimensions, with slightly larger gains on mid-complexity\\n3. For 8B, RSFT gain is notably larger on long docs (+4.6pt vs +2.3pt short/mid), suggesting RSFT helps more with complex multi-sentence reasoning at larger scale\\n4. Per-relation analysis shows RSFT gains are relation-type-dependent, not just a uniform improvement — useful for paper narrative about what RSFT learns\\n\\n## Per-Relation Analysis: 1.7B Qwen & LLaMA-8B (2026-05-19)\\n\\n**Files**: `scripts/analysis/results/per_relation_1_7b.{txt,csv}`, `per_relation_llama.{txt,csv}`\\n\\n**1.7B Qwen (SFT → RSFT)**:\\n- micro-F1: 0.4945 → 0.5290 (+3.45pp)\\n- 89 high-freq relations: 55 improved (+0.053 avg), 30 degraded (-0.047 avg)\\n- Top gains: screenwriter (+0.172), conflict (+0.133), notable work (+0.127)\\n- Top losses: founded by (-0.186), participant of (-0.166), cast member (-0.109)\\n\\n**LLaMA-8B (SFT → RSFT)**:\\n- micro-F1: 0.5514 → 0.5839 (+3.25pp)\\n- 89 high-freq relations: 57 improved (+0.052 avg), 26 degraded (-0.061 avg)\\n- Top gains: operator (+0.305), participant of (+0.163), mother/screenwriter (+0.114)\\n- Top losses: genre (-0.301), chairperson (-0.267), capital of (-0.152)\\n- **Hallucinated relations (0 gold, FP only): 25 types vs 1.7B Qwen's 6 types**\\n\\n### Cross-Model Insights\\n- RSFT improvement magnitude is consistent across model families (~3.3-3.5pp micro-F1)\\n- Both models show majority improvement (55-57/89 relations gain)\\n- LLaMA degradation per-relation is sharper (-0.061 avg vs -0.047) despite improving more relations\\n- **LLaMA has 4x more hallucinated relation types** — suggests Qwen family is more disciplined in relation space, LLaMA more prone to inventing non-existent relations\\n- \\\"participant of\\\" flips between models: degraded in 1.7B Qwen (-0.166), improved in LLaMA-8B (+0.163) — model family × relation interaction\\n\\n## plan_020: Evidence Tax Mechanism Analysis (2026-05-19)\\n\\n**Files**: `scripts/analysis/evidence_tax_analysis.py`, `scripts/analysis/evidence_tax_plots.py` → `scripts/analysis/results/`\\n\\n**Pairs analyzed** (all dev set, SFT models):\\n- Qwen3-1.7B: `qwen3_1_7b_sft_eval` (F1=0.494) vs `qwen3_1_7b_no_evidence_eval_v3` (F1=0.410)\\n- Qwen3-4B: `d111_4b_sft_s42_reeval` (F1=0.405) vs `plan_014_no_evidence_eval_d076` (F1=0.488)\\n- Qwen3-8B: `qwen3_8b_sft_eval` (F1=0.526) vs `qwen3_8b_no_evidence_eval_v2` (F1=0.528)\\n- Llama-3.1-8B: `llama_3_1_8b_sft_eval` (F1=0.551) vs `llama_3_1_8b_no_evidence_eval` (F1=0.562)\\n\\n### Key Findings\\n\\n**1. Evidence tax is non-monotonic across scale:**\\n| Scale | With-Ev F1 | No-Ev F1 | Tax (pp) | Direction |\\n|-------|-----------|----------|----------|-----------|\\n| 1.7B  | 0.494     | 0.410    | +8.4     | with-ev better |\\n| 4B    | 0.405     | 0.488    | -8.3     | no-ev better |\\n| 8B Q  | 0.526     | 0.528    | -0.2     | ~tied |\\n| 8B L  | 0.551     | 0.562    | -1.1     | no-ev slightly better |\\n\\n**2. Truncation is NOT the primary mechanism:**\\n- 1.7B: no-evidence has HIGHER truncation (50.9% vs 31.9%) — opposite of naive hypothesis\\n- 4B: similar truncation rates (29.2% vs 31.2%)\\n- 8B: with-evidence slightly more truncated (31.8% vs 22.8%)\\n\\n**3. Evidence as regularizer at small scale (1.7B paradox):**\\n- 1.7B no-evidence generates 26K total predictions vs 17K with-evidence\\n- 1.7B no-evidence has 60.5% duplicate triples vs 33.0% with-evidence\\n- Without evidence constraints, the 1.7B model over-generates and repeats triples, wasting token budget\\n- Per-doc F1 wins: with-ev=571, no-ev=283 — with-evidence wins on 58% of documents\\n\\n**4. Evidence character overhead:**\\n- Evidence fields consume ~20% of output chars across all Qwen3 scales\\n- Per-triple cost: with-ev ~102 chars/triple vs no-ev ~85 chars/triple (1.2x ratio)\\n- Llama-3.1-8B is the exception: 23% overhead but per-triple ratio is 1.00x (Llama uses longer relation names)\\n\\n**5. False positive patterns:**\\n- No-evidence models consistently have higher FP rates at all scales except 4B (4B with-ev has anomalously high FP rate at 58.7%)\\n- 1.7B no-ev FP rate: 55.3% vs with-ev 49.1%\\n\\n**6. Duplicate triples are a major token waste:**\\n- All no-evidence models have higher duplication rates than with-evidence counterparts\\n- 1.7B no-ev: 60.5% duplicates (15814/26157 triples are redundant copies)\\n- Evidence field provides a de-duplication signal: same (h,r,t) with different evidence gets generated, but at least carries distinct information\\n\\n### Interpretation for Paper\\n1. Evidence requirement does NOT simply \\\"tax\\\" by consuming output space — the mechanism is more nuanced\\n2. At small scale (1.7B), evidence acts as structural regularization, preventing degenerate repetitive generation\\n3. At medium scale (4B), the 4B with-evidence model appears to be a weaker adapter (possibly training artifact, not inherent evidence mechanism). Need to verify with matched training conditions.\\n4. At large scale (8B+), evidence tax is negligible (~0-1pp), suggesting larger models handle the additional output complexity without degradation\\n5. The dominant mechanism behind 1.7B's \\\"negative tax\\\" is duplicate suppression and generation discipline, not output length\\n\\n## D116: QLoRA Confound 教训 (2026-05-19)\\n\\n**决策**: D112 RSFT 训练误用了 --quantize（QLoRA），导致 adapter 适应 4-bit 量化误差。bf16 eval 下 ign_f1=0.2181（灾难性崩溃）。\\n\\n**教训**: 所有 RSFT 训练必须使用 bf16 LoRA（与 canonical 实验一致）。训练脚本中不得出现 --quantize 参数。QLoRA adapter 与 bf16 backbone 不兼容——adapter 学到的是如何补偿量化误差，去掉量化后补偿变成噪声。\\n\\n**行动**: bf16 重跑 (d112_rsft_s44_train_bf16) 已提交 cuda:0。\\n\", \"total_chars\": 8741, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
